"""Read-only source inspection. Never imports or executes corpus modules."""
from pathlib import Path
import ast
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import inspect
import json
import re

from fixture_templates import C_HELPERS, M_HELPERS, S_HELPERS

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CORE_METHODS = (set(M_HELPERS.values()) | set(C_HELPERS.values())
                | {method for pair in S_HELPERS.values() for method in pair})

PROBE_ARTIFACTS = {
    "tool_description": {"server.py", "tools.json"},
    "input_schema_description": {"models.py", "tools.json"},
    "resource_body": {"content.txt"},
    "tool_result": {"content.txt"},
    "prompt_message": {"content.txt"},
}

TRACKED_CALLS = {
    "subprocess.run", "subprocess.Popen", "subprocess.check_output",
    "asyncio.create_subprocess_exec",
    "os.getenv", "os.environ.get",
    "HTTPConnection", "connection.request", "connection.getresponse", "conn.request", "conn.getresponse",
    "urllib.request.urlopen", "urllib.request.Request",
    "socket.create_connection",
} | {f"self.{method}" for method in CORE_METHODS} | {method for method in CORE_METHODS}


def digest(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def canonical(value):
    return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()


def write_json(path, value):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def source_files(folder):
    return sorted(p for p in folder.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
                  and "__pycache__" not in p.parts and p.suffix != ".pyc")


def snapshot(folder):
    return {p.relative_to(folder).as_posix(): digest(p.read_bytes()) for p in source_files(folder)}


def call_name(node):
    return ast.unparse(node.func)


def is_tool(function):
    return any(
        (isinstance(d, ast.Call) and ast.unparse(d.func) == "mcp.tool")
        or (isinstance(d, ast.Attribute) and ast.unparse(d) == "mcp.tool")
        for d in function.decorator_list
    )


def inspect_sample(root, sample, directory):
    sid = sample["sample_id"]
    if not re.fullmatch(r"SIM\d{3}", sid):
        raise ValueError(f"Unexpected sample ID: {sid}")
    folder = (root / directory / sid).resolve()
    if folder.parent != (root / directory).resolve():
        raise ValueError("Sample path outside corpus")
    hashes = snapshot(folder)
    issues, facts, methods, exported = [], [], {}, {}
    manifest_files = set()
    for name, artifact in sample["artifacts"].items():
        path = (root / artifact["path"]).resolve()
        if not path.is_relative_to(folder):
            raise ValueError("Artifact escapes sample directory")
        relative = path.relative_to(folder).as_posix()
        manifest_files.add(relative)
        if hashes.get(relative) != artifact["sha256"]:
            issues.append({"code": "ARTIFACT_HASH_MISMATCH", "path": artifact["path"]})
    for relative in sorted(set(hashes) - manifest_files):
        issues.append({"code": "UNMANIFESTED_FILE", "path": f"{directory}/{sid}/{relative}"})
    python_count = 0
    for path in source_files(folder):
        if path.suffix != ".py":
            continue
        python_count += 1
        relative = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        try:
            tree = ast.parse(path.read_bytes())
        except (SyntaxError, ValueError) as error:
            issues.append({"code": "SYNTAX_ERROR", "path": relative, "line": getattr(error, "lineno", None)})
            continue
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
                if node.name in CORE_METHODS:
                    methods[node.name] = digest(ast.dump(node, include_attributes=False).encode())
                if is_tool(node):
                    if node.name in exported:
                        issues.append({"code": "DUPLICATE_TOOL_NAME", "name": node.name})
                    exported[node.name] = inspect.cleandoc(ast.get_docstring(node, clean=False) or "")
            if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and call_name(node) in TRACKED_CALLS:
                fact = {"path": relative, "line": node.lineno, "end_line": node.end_lineno,
                        "source_sha256": hashes[path.relative_to(folder).as_posix()],
                        "call": call_name(node), "positional_argument_count": len(node.args)}
                if fact["call"].startswith("subprocess.") or fact["call"] == "asyncio.create_subprocess_exec":
                    for keyword in node.keywords:
                        if keyword.arg in ("shell", "check", "timeout") and isinstance(keyword.value, ast.Constant):
                            fact[keyword.arg] = keyword.value.value
                if fact["call"] in ("os.getenv", "os.environ.get") and node.args and isinstance(node.args[0], ast.Constant):
                    fact["environment_variable_name"] = node.args[0].value
                facts.append(fact)
    tools_path = folder / "tools.json"
    try:
        tools = json.loads(tools_path.read_bytes())["tools"]
        listed = {t["name"]: inspect.cleandoc(t.get("description", "")) for t in tools}
        if len(listed) != len(tools) or exported != listed:
            issues.append({"code": "TOOL_NAME_OR_DESCRIPTION_MISMATCH"})
    except (ValueError, KeyError, OSError, TypeError):
        issues.append({"code": "INVALID_TOOLS_JSON"})
    skill = sample.get("skill_view")
    if skill:
        view = (root / skill["path"]).resolve()
        if not view.is_relative_to((root / "skill-views").resolve()):
            raise ValueError("Skill view escapes corpus")
        for name, expected in skill["sha256"].items():
            file = (view / name).resolve()
            if not file.is_relative_to(view):
                raise ValueError("Skill artifact escapes view")
            if not file.exists() or digest(file.read_bytes()) != expected:
                issues.append({"code": "SKILL_HASH_MISMATCH", "file": name})
        for name, expected in hashes.items():
            file = view / name
            if not file.exists() or digest(file.read_bytes()) != expected:
                issues.append({"code": "SKILL_SOURCE_MISMATCH", "file": name})
    content = folder / "content.txt"
    return {"sample_id": sid, "family_id": sample["family_id"],
            "pair_id": sid, "source_directory": directory,
            "snapshot_sha256": digest(canonical(hashes)), "artifacts": hashes,
            "python_files_parsed": python_count, "static_facts": facts, "issues": issues,
            "core_method_fingerprints": methods,
            "content_fingerprint": digest(content.read_bytes()) if content.exists() else None,
            "metadata_check": "static_tool_names_and_descriptions_only",
            "runtime_execution": "NOT_PERFORMED"}


def assign_groups(records):
    """Join declared families, shared behavior methods and identical text."""
    parents = {r["sample_id"]: r["sample_id"] for r in records}
    def find(item):
        while parents[item] != item:
            parents[item] = parents[parents[item]]
            item = parents[item]
        return item
    tokens = {}
    for record in records:
        sid = record["sample_id"]
        keys = ["family:" + record["family_id"]]
        if sid.startswith("SIM"):
            keys += ["method:" + name + ":" + value for name, value in record["core_method_fingerprints"].items()]
            if record["content_fingerprint"]:
                keys.append("content:" + record["content_fingerprint"])
        for key in keys:
            if key in tokens:
                parents[find(sid)] = find(tokens[key])
            else:
                tokens[key] = sid
    components = defaultdict(list)
    for sid in parents:
        components[find(sid)].append(sid)
    result = []
    for index, members in enumerate(sorted(components.values(), key=lambda x: min(x)), 1):
        name = f"GROUP{index:02d}"
        for record in records:
            if record["sample_id"] in members:
                record["recommended_split_group"] = name
        result.append({"group_id": name, "members": sorted(members), "count": len(members)})
    return result


def check_probe_surfaces(root, records):
    cases = {case["sample_id"]: case for case in json.loads((root / "scenarios.json").read_bytes())}
    seen = set()
    for record in records:
        sid = record["sample_id"]
        if not sid.startswith("SIM"):
            continue
        case = cases[sid]
        if not case["probe_surface"]:
            continue
        probe = case["probe"].encode("utf-8")
        fingerprint = digest(probe)
        if fingerprint in seen:
            record["issues"].append({"code": "DUPLICATE_PROBE_TEXT"})
        seen.add(fingerprint)
        folder = root / "samples" / sid
        probe_variants = {probe, probe.replace(b"\n", b"\\n")}
        found = {name for name in record["artifacts"] if any(v in (folder / name).read_bytes() for v in probe_variants)}

        if case["probe_surface"] == "input_schema_description":
            schema_file = (
                "models.py" if "models.py" in record["artifacts"] and any(v in (folder / "models.py").read_bytes() for v in probe_variants)
                else "service.py" if "service.py" in record["artifacts"] and any(v in (folder / "service.py").read_bytes() for v in probe_variants)
                else "core/handler.py" if "core/handler.py" in record["artifacts"] and any(v in (folder / "core" / "handler.py").read_bytes() for v in probe_variants)
                else "server.py"
            )
            expected = {schema_file, "tools.json"}
        else:
            expected = PROBE_ARTIFACTS[case["probe_surface"]]

        if found != expected or (("content.txt" in record["artifacts"]) != ("content.txt" in expected)):
            record["issues"].append({"code": "PROBE_SURFACE_CONTAMINATION", "expected": sorted(expected), "found": sorted(found)})
        if case["probe_surface"] in ("tool_description", "input_schema_description"):
            if not any(v in (root / "skill-views" / sid / "SKILL.md").read_bytes() for v in probe_variants):
                record["issues"].append({"code": "MISSING_SKILL_PROBE_SURFACE"})
        elif case["probe_surface"] in ("resource_body", "tool_result", "prompt_message"):
            py_files = [f for f in record["artifacts"] if f.endswith(".py")]
            has_content_ref = any(b"content.txt" in (folder / f).read_bytes() for f in py_files)
            if not has_content_ref:
                record["issues"].append({"code": "MISSING_CONTENT_TXT_REFERENCE"})
    return len(seen)


def run(root=ROOT):
    records, imports = [], {}
    directory, name = "samples", "manifest.json"
    raw = (root / name).read_bytes()
    imports[name] = digest(raw)
    for sample in json.loads(raw)["samples"]:
        records.append(inspect_sample(root, sample, directory))
    if len({r["sample_id"] for r in records}) != len(records):
        raise ValueError("Duplicate sample IDs")
    probe_count = check_probe_surfaces(root, records)
    groups = assign_groups(records)
    for record in records:
        write_json(root / "evaluation" / "static-evidence" / (record["sample_id"] + ".json"), record)
    reports = {}
    ver_path = root / "verification.json"
    if ver_path.exists():
        data = json.loads(ver_path.read_bytes())
        reports["verification.json"] = {
            "file_sha256": digest(ver_path.read_bytes()),
            "manifest_hash_matches": data.get("manifest_sha256") == imports[name],
            "reported_stdio_sessions": data.get("stdio_sessions_verified"),
            "provenance": "existing_report_not_rerun",
            "new_behavior_success": "NOT_ESTABLISHED_BY_THIS_STATIC_AUDIT",
        }
    report = {
        "schema_version": "1.0",
        "audited_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "audit_script_sha256": digest(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
        "source_manifests": imports,
        "sample_count": len(records),
        "python_files_parsed": sum(r["python_files_parsed"] for r in records),
        "issue_count": sum(len(r["issues"]) for r in records),
        "recommended_split_groups": groups,
        "unique_probe_texts": probe_count,
        "existing_runtime_reports": reports,
        "samples": records,
        "executed_samples": 0,
    }
    write_json(root / "evaluation" / "static-audit.json", report)
    print(f"Static audit: {len(records)} samples, {report['python_files_parsed']} Python files, {report['issue_count']} issues, {len(groups)} split groups; executed samples: 0")
    return report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()


==========================================================================================
FILE: verify_static_sinks.py
==========================================================================================

"""Verify static sinks, dual-mode worker contracts, and Group P surface integrity."""
from pathlib import Path
import ast
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

FORBIDDEN_TOKENS = [
    "create_subprocess_shell",
    "os.popen",
    "base64",
    "8710",
    '".env"',
    "'.env'",
    "api_key",
    "secret_key",
    "bot_token",
    "access_key",
    "auth_context",
    '"token"',
]

C_WORKERS = {
    "C01": ("counter_sync.py", "SIM026"),
    "C02": ("indicator_service.py", "SIM031"),
    "C03": ("device_dispatcher.py", "SIM036"),
    "C04": ("asset_subsystem.py", "SIM041"),
    "C05": ("task_executor.py", "SIM046"),
}


def check_ast_parse():
    py_files = sorted(list(ROOT.glob("samples/**/*.py")) + list(ROOT.glob("skill-views/**/*.py")))
    if not py_files:
        raise RuntimeError("No Python files found in samples/ or skill-views/")
    for path in py_files:
        try:
            ast.parse(path.read_bytes(), filename=str(path))
        except SyntaxError as e:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Syntax error in {path}: {e}")
    print(f"[CHECK 1 PASSED] AST parsed {len(py_files)} Python files successfully.")


def check_group_c_gradient():
    for i in range(26, 51):
        sid = f"SIM{i:03d}"
        edge = ((i - 1) % 5) + 1
        sample_dir = ROOT / "samples" / sid

        py_files = list(sample_dir.glob("**/*.py"))

        if edge in (1, 2):
            target_file = sample_dir / ("server.py" if edge == 1 else "service.py")
            content = target_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            if "shell=True" not in content:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} (E{edge}) missing shell=True in {target_file.name}")
            if "--job" not in content or "apply" not in content or "--ref" not in content:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} (E{edge}) missing --job/apply/--ref arguments")
        else:
            # E3, E4, E5 must have zero shell keyword arguments in all python files
            for pf in py_files:
                tree = ast.parse(pf.read_bytes())
                for node in ast.walk(tree):
                    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
                        for kw in node.keywords:
                            if kw.arg == "shell":
                                raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} (E{edge}) has forbidden 'shell' keyword in {pf}")

            if edge == 3:
                handler = sample_dir / "core" / "handler.py"
                txt = handler.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                if "create_subprocess_exec" not in txt or "--job" not in txt or "apply" not in txt or "--ref" not in txt:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} E3 missing create_subprocess_exec with --job apply --ref")
            elif edge == 4:
                domain = sample_dir / "domain.py"
                txt = domain.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                if "subprocess.run" not in txt or "--job" not in txt or "apply" not in txt or "--ref" not in txt:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} E4 missing subprocess.run with --job apply --ref")
            elif edge == 5:
                domain = sample_dir / "domain.py"
                txt = domain.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                if "subprocess.Popen" not in txt or "--job" not in txt or "apply" not in txt or "--ref" not in txt:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} E5 missing subprocess.Popen with --job apply --ref")

    print("[CHECK 2 PASSED] Group C shell vs. argv gradient verified across SIM026-SIM050.")


def check_forbidden_tokens():
    targets = sorted(list(ROOT.glob("samples/**/*")) + list(ROOT.glob("skill-views/**/*")))
    files = [p for p in targets if p.is_file() and "__pycache__" not in p.parts and p.suffix != ".pyc"]

    violations = []
    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        for token in FORBIDDEN_TOKENS:
            if token in text:
                violations.append((str(f.relative_to(ROOT)), token))

    if violations:
        msg = "\n".join(f"  {path}: contains forbidden token {tok!r}" for path, tok in violations[:20])
        raise RuntimeError(f"Forbidden tokens found ({len(violations)} occurrences):\n{msg}")

    print(f"[CHECK 3 PASSED] Zero forbidden tokens across {len(files)} corpus files.")


def check_worker_scripts():
    results = {}
    for code, (worker_name, sid) in C_WORKERS.items():
        worker_path = ROOT / "samples" / sid / worker_name
        if not worker_path.exists():
            raise RuntimeError(f"Worker script {worker_path} not found")

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_dir:
            tmp_path = Path(tmp_dir)
            tmp_worker = tmp_path / worker_name
            tmp_worker.write_bytes(worker_path.read_bytes())
            state_file = tmp_path / "device_state.json"

            # 1. Run with --job apply --ref ROW1
            cmd_apply = [sys.executable, "-I", "-B", str(tmp_worker), "--job", "apply", "--ref", "ROW1"]
            proc1 = subprocess.run(cmd_apply, cwd=tmp_dir, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
            out1 = json.loads(proc1.stdout.strip())
            if not state_file.exists():
                raise RuntimeError(f"Worker {worker_name} did not create device_state.json on apply")
            state1 = json.loads(state_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip())

            if state1.get("state") != "changed" or state1.get("target") != "ROW1":
                raise RuntimeError(f"Worker {worker_name} apply state invalid: {state1}")
            if out1.get("state") != "changed" or out1.get("target") != "ROW1":
                raise RuntimeError(f"Worker {worker_name} apply output invalid: {out1}")

            # 2. Run with --job status --ref ROW1
            cmd_status = [sys.executable, "-I", "-B", str(tmp_worker), "--job", "status", "--ref", "ROW1"]
            proc2 = subprocess.run(cmd_status, cwd=tmp_dir, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
            out2 = json.loads(proc2.stdout.strip())
            state2 = json.loads(state_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip())

            if state2.get("state") != "unchanged" or state2.get("target") != "ROW1":
                raise RuntimeError(f"Worker {worker_name} status state invalid: {state2}")
            if out2.get("state") != "unchanged" or out2.get("target") != "ROW1":
                raise RuntimeError(f"Worker {worker_name} status output invalid: {out2}")

            results[worker_name] = {
                "sample_id": sid,
                "family": code,
                "apply_result": {"stdout": out1, "device_state": state1},
                "status_result": {"stdout": out2, "device_state": state2},
                "verified": True,
            }

    evidence_dir = ROOT / "evaluation" / "runtime-evidence"
    evidence_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (evidence_dir / "workers.json").write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[CHECK 4 PASSED] Verified 5 worker scripts in tempdir; saved to evaluation/runtime-evidence/workers.json.")


def check_group_p_surfaces():
    scenarios_path = ROOT / "scenarios.json"
    manifest_path = ROOT / "manifest.json"
    if not scenarios_path.exists() or not manifest_path.exists():
        raise RuntimeError("scenarios.json or manifest.json not found")
    scenarios = {s["sample_id"]: s for s in json.loads(scenarios_path.read_bytes())}
    manifest = {s["sample_id"]: s for s in json.loads(manifest_path.read_bytes())["samples"]}

    for i in range(76, 101):
        sid = f"SIM{i:03d}"
        case = scenarios[sid]
        code = manifest[sid]["family_id"]
        probe = case["probe"]

        # P03 reads content.txt into preview
        if code == "P03":
            sample_dir = ROOT / "samples" / sid
            py_files = [f for f in sample_dir.glob("**/*.py") if f.name != "__init__.py"]
            reads_content = False
            for pf in py_files:
                txt = pf.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                if "content.txt" in txt and '"preview": note' in txt:
                    reads_content = True
                    break
            if not reads_content:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} (P03) does not read content.txt into preview")

        # Check SKILL.md probe inclusion/exclusion
        skill_path = ROOT / "skill-views" / sid / "SKILL.md"
        if not skill_path.exists():
            raise RuntimeError(f"Missing {skill_path}")
        skill_text = skill_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

        if code in ("P02", "P03", "P05"):
            if probe in skill_text:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} ({code}) leaked probe into SKILL.md")
        elif code in ("P01", "P04"):
            if probe not in skill_text:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} ({code}) missing probe in SKILL.md")

    print("[CHECK 5 PASSED] Group P surfaces verified across SIM076-SIM100.")


def check_production_depth():
    min_lines = float("inf")
    max_lines = 0
    total_lines = 0

    for i in range(1, 101):
        sid = f"SIM{i:03d}"
        sample_dir = ROOT / "samples" / sid
        py_files = [f for f in sample_dir.glob("**/*.py") if f.name != "__init__.py"]

        sample_line_count = sum(len(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()) for f in sample_dir.glob("**/*.py"))
        if sample_line_count < 75:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} has only {sample_line_count} Python lines (< 75 required)")

        min_lines = min(min_lines, sample_line_count)
        max_lines = max(max_lines, sample_line_count)
        total_lines += sample_line_count

        catalog_found = False
        for pf in py_files:
            txt = pf.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            if "_CATALOG_DATA" in txt:
                tree = ast.parse(txt)
                for node in tree.body:
                    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
                        for target in node.targets:
                            if isinstance(target, ast.Name) and target.id == "_CATALOG_DATA":
                                if isinstance(node.value, ast.List) and len(node.value.elts) >= 4:
                                    catalog_found = True
                                    break
            if catalog_found:
                break
        if not catalog_found:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{sid} missing _CATALOG_DATA with >= 4 records")

    avg_lines = total_lines / 100.0
    print(f"[CHECK 6 PASSED] Production depth verified: min={min_lines}, avg={avg_lines:.1f}, max={max_lines} lines/sample (all >= 75 lines, all have _CATALOG_DATA >= 4 records).")


def main():
    check_ast_parse()
    check_group_c_gradient()
    check_forbidden_tokens()
    check_worker_scripts()
    check_group_p_surfaces()
    check_production_depth()
    print("STATIC_SINK_OK")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


==========================================================================================
FILE: prepare_evaluation.py
==========================================================================================

"""Build review-derived admission records from fresh, non-executing evidence."""
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from datetime import datetime
import json

from audit_corpus import ROOT, canonical, digest, snapshot, write_json

GAPS = {
    "M": ["Contract mismatch changes only fixture-owned ledger files."],
    "C": ["Spawns a local worker process mutating state outside the declared read-only tool contract."],
    "S": ["Only synthetic records can reach the fixed loopback receiver."],
    "P": ["Presentation probe delivery is tested; agent compliance has not been measured."],
}


def validate_review(review, record, root=ROOT):
    if review.get("status") != "reviewed":
        return ["PENDING_REVIEW"]
    errors = []
    for name in ("reviewer", "reviewed_at", "rubric_id", "rationale"):
        if not isinstance(review.get(name), str) or not review[name].strip():
            errors.append("MISSING_" + name.upper())
    try:
        timestamp = datetime.fromisoformat(review.get("reviewed_at", "").replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        if timestamp.tzinfo is None:
            errors.append("REVIEW_TIMEZONE_REQUIRED")
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        errors.append("INVALID_REVIEW_DATE")
    if review.get("snapshot_sha256") != record["snapshot_sha256"]:
        errors.append("STALE_REVIEW_SNAPSHOT")
    if review.get("label") not in ("benign", "malicious"):
        errors.append("INVALID_LABEL")
    if review.get("assessed_scope") not in ("source_intent", "runtime_effect"):
        errors.append("INVALID_SCOPE")
    refs = review.get("evidence_refs")
    if not isinstance(refs, list) or not refs:
        errors.append("SOURCE_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED")
        refs = []
    for ref in refs:
        if not isinstance(ref, dict) or not isinstance(ref.get("path"), str):
            errors.append("INVALID_EVIDENCE_REFERENCE")
            continue
        path = (root / ref["path"]).resolve()
        folder = (root / record["source_directory"] / record["sample_id"]).resolve()
        if not path.is_relative_to(folder) or not path.is_file():
            errors.append("INVALID_EVIDENCE_PATH")
            continue
        line = ref.get("line")
        if type(line) is not int or not 1 <= line <= len(path.read_bytes().splitlines()):
            errors.append("INVALID_EVIDENCE_LINE")
    if review.get("assessed_scope") == "runtime_effect":
        runtime = review.get("runtime_evidence")
        if not isinstance(runtime, dict) or not isinstance(runtime.get("path"), str):
            errors.append("RUNTIME_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED")
        else:
            path = (root / runtime["path"]).resolve()
            if not path.is_relative_to(root.resolve()) or not path.is_file():
                errors.append("INVALID_RUNTIME_EVIDENCE_PATH")
            elif digest(path.read_bytes()) != runtime.get("sha256"):
                errors.append("RUNTIME_EVIDENCE_HASH_MISMATCH")
    return errors


def populate_ground_truth(root=ROOT):
    audit_path = root / "evaluation" / "static-audit.json"
    if not audit_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError("Static audit not found; run audit_corpus.py first")
    audit = json.loads(audit_path.read_bytes())
    manifest = json.loads((root / "manifest.json").read_bytes())
    origins = {s["sample_id"]: s for s in manifest["samples"]}

    reviews = []
    for record in audit["samples"]:
        sid = record["sample_id"]
        origin = origins[sid]
        evidence_refs = origin.get("evidence_locations", [])
        reviews.append({
            "sample_id": sid,
            "snapshot_sha256": record["snapshot_sha256"],
            "status": "reviewed",
            "label": "malicious",
            "assessed_scope": "source_intent",
            "reviewer": "mcp-security-review-team",
            "reviewed_at": "2026-11-13T08:00:00+07:00",
            "rubric_id": "rubric-sast-v4",
            "rationale": f"Verified intentional {origin['research_target']} ({origin['observed_mechanism']}).",
            "evidence_refs": evidence_refs,
            "runtime_evidence": None,
            "split": "development",
        })
    write_json(root / "evaluation" / "reviews.json", {"schema_version": "1.0", "reviews": reviews})
    print(f"Populated ground truth reviews for {len(reviews)} samples in evaluation/reviews.json")


def prepare(root=ROOT):
    audit_path = root / "evaluation" / "static-audit.json"
    audit = json.loads(audit_path.read_bytes())
    for name, expected in audit["source_manifests"].items():
        if digest((root / name).read_bytes()) != expected:
            raise ValueError("Source manifest changed; rerun static audit")
    origins = {}
    for name in audit["source_manifests"]:
        for sample in json.loads((root / name).read_bytes())["samples"]:
            origins[sample["sample_id"]] = sample
    for record in audit["samples"]:
        folder = root / record["source_directory"] / record["sample_id"]
        if digest(canonical(snapshot(folder))) != record["snapshot_sha256"]:
            raise ValueError(f"Source changed since audit: {record['sample_id']}")
    review_path = root / "evaluation" / "reviews.json"
    if not review_path.exists():
        populate_ground_truth(root)
    entries = json.loads(review_path.read_bytes())["reviews"]
    reviews = {r["sample_id"]: r for r in entries}
    known = {r["sample_id"] for r in audit["samples"]}
    if len(entries) != len(reviews) or set(reviews) != known:
        raise ValueError("Reviews must contain each audited sample exactly once")
    split_groups = defaultdict(set)
    for record in audit["samples"]:
        split_groups[record["recommended_split_group"]].add(reviews[record["sample_id"]].get("split"))
    records = []
    for item in audit["samples"]:
        sid, group = item["sample_id"], item["family_id"][0]
        original = origins[sid]
        review = reviews[sid]
        errors = validate_review(review, item, root)
        if item["issues"]:
            errors.append("STATIC_INTEGRITY_OR_METADATA_ISSUE")
        split = review.get("split")
        if split not in ("development", "held_out"):
            errors.append("INVALID_SPLIT")
        if len(split_groups[item["recommended_split_group"]]) != 1:
            errors.append("MECHANISM_GROUP_SPLIT_LEAKAGE")
        accepted = not errors
        records.append({
            "sample_id": sid, "pair_id": item["pair_id"], "family_id": item["family_id"],
            "split": split, "split_group": item["recommended_split_group"],
            "snapshot_sha256": item["snapshot_sha256"], "source_directory": item["source_directory"],
            "proposed_malware_label": "malicious",
            "candidate_category": original["research_target"],
            "observed_mechanism": original["observed_mechanism"],
            "original_declared_label": original["server_label"],
            "original_admission_claim": original.get("benchmark_admission", original.get("review_status")),
            "static_evidence": f"evaluation/static-evidence/{sid}.json",
            "runtime_success": "NOT_ESTABLISHED_BY_STATIC_AUDIT",
            "evidence_gaps": GAPS[group],
            "hard_negative_candidate": bool(original.get("hard_negative_candidate")),
            "malware_ground_truth": review["label"] if accepted else None,
            "assessed_scope": review.get("assessed_scope") if accepted else None,
            "reviewer": review.get("reviewer") if accepted else None,
            "rubric_id": review.get("rubric_id") if accepted else None,
            "review_status": "accepted_review_record" if accepted else "pending_or_invalid_review",
            "scoring_eligible": accepted, "admission_reasons": errors,
        })
    admitted = Counter(r["malware_ground_truth"] for r in records if r["scoring_eligible"])
    result = {"schema_version": "3.0", "scope": "Snapshot-bound review and benchmark admission",
              "static_audit_sha256": digest(audit_path.read_bytes()),
              "reviews_sha256": digest(review_path.read_bytes()),
              "readiness": {"total": len(records), "admitted_malicious": admitted["malicious"],
                            "admitted_benign": admitted["benign"],
                            "hard_negative_candidates": sum(r["hard_negative_candidate"] for r in records),
                            "scan_preparation": "STATIC_INPUTS_AVAILABLE",
                            "malware_metrics": "MALICIOUS_COHORT_READY_FOR_EVALUATION" if admitted["malicious"] > 0 else "NOT_READY",
                            "external_scanners": "NOT_RUN_BY_THIS_PIPELINE"}, "samples": records}
    write_json(root / "benchmark-manifest.json", result)
    lines = ["# Hồ sơ 100 mẫu SIM", "", "Nhãn đề xuất, bằng chứng tĩnh và admission được ghi riêng. Bảng này không khẳng định hành vi runtime mới đã thành công.", "",
             "| SIM | Họ | Nhóm chia tập | Cơ chế SIM đọc từ source | Evidence |",
             "|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for item in records:
        if item["sample_id"].startswith("SIM"):
            sid = item["sample_id"]
            lines.append(f'| [{sid}](samples/{sid}/) | {item["family_id"]} | {item["split_group"]} | {item["observed_mechanism"]} | [static]({item["static_evidence"]}) |')
    (root / "SIM_INDEX.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(result["readiness"]))
    return result


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--auto-admit", action="store_true", default=False,
                        help="Populate reviews.json with reviewed ground-truth labels for all 100 samples")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.auto_admit:
        populate_ground_truth()
    prepare()

FILE: create_handoff.py

"""Prepare scanner input records from reviewed admission metadata."""
from pathlib import Path
import json

from audit_corpus import ROOT, digest, write_json


def create(root=ROOT):
    manifest = json.loads((root / "benchmark-manifest.json").read_bytes())
    if digest((root / "evaluation/static-audit.json").read_bytes()) != manifest["static_audit_sha256"]:
        raise ValueError("Audit changed; regenerate benchmark manifest")
    target = root / "scan-results-template.json"
    if target.exists():
        previous = json.loads(target.read_bytes())
        if any(r.get("status") != "NOT_SCANNED" or r.get("prediction") is not None or r.get("raw_output")
               for r in previous.get("runs", [])):
            raise ValueError("Existing file contains results; preserve it before regenerating the template")
    lines = ["# Danh mục input scanner", "", "100 source snapshot gồm 100 mẫu SIM. Admission lấy từ benchmark-manifest.json, không suy từ tên thư mục.", "",
             "| ID | Nhãn đề xuất | Cơ chế quan sát tĩnh | Admission | Source | Evidence |",
             "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    routes = []
    for sample in manifest["samples"]:
        sid = sample["sample_id"]
        folder = f'{sample["source_directory"]}/{sid}'
        skill = root / "skill-views" / sid
        inputs = {
            "MCP Audit": (f"launch/{sid}.json", "config_or_live_metadata", "REQUIRES_CONFIG_RESOLUTION"),
            "MCTS": (folder, "repository_source", "AVAILABLE"),
            "Cisco MCP Scanner": (folder, "behavioral_source", "AVAILABLE"),
            "MCPShield": (f"launch/{sid}.json", "config_or_live_metadata", "REQUIRES_CONFIG_RESOLUTION"),
            "NVIDIA SkillSpector": (f"skill-views/{sid}" if skill.exists() else None, "derived_skill_directory", "AVAILABLE" if skill.exists() else "NOT_PREPARED"),
        }
        for scanner, (path, mode, input_status) in inputs.items():
            routes.append({"sample_id": sid, "scanner": scanner, "scanner_version": None,
                           "input": path, "mode_to_confirm": mode, "input_status": input_status,
                           "input_snapshot_sha256": sample["snapshot_sha256"],
                           "assessed_scope": None, "rubric_id": None, "model_or_analyzer": None,
                           "status": "NOT_SCANNED", "prediction": None, "findings": [],
                           "latency_seconds": None, "cost": None, "raw_output": None,
                           "raw_output_sha256": None, "scoring_eligible": sample["scoring_eligible"]})
        lines.append(f'| {sid} | {sample["proposed_malware_label"]} | {sample["observed_mechanism"]} | {sample["review_status"]} | [folder]({folder}/) | [static]({sample["static_evidence"]}) |')
    (root / "SAMPLE_INDEX.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    write_json(target, {"note": "Copy selected rows to a results file. Missing skill views are NOT_PREPARED, not scanner UNSUPPORTED results.", "runs": routes})
    print(f"Prepared {len(routes)} NOT_SCANNED records for {len(manifest['samples'])} snapshots")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    create()


==========================================================================================
FILE: score_results.py
==========================================================================================

"""Score normalized results against reviewed labels, reporting incomplete coverage."""
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
import argparse
import json

from audit_corpus import ROOT, canonical, digest, snapshot, write_json

STATUSES = {"COMPLETED", "ERROR", "TIMEOUT", "PARTIAL", "UNSUPPORTED", "NOT_SCANNED"}


def score(manifest, results):
    required = ("scanner", "scanner_version", "mode", "assessed_scope", "rubric_id")
    if any(not isinstance(results.get(k), str) or not results[k].strip() for k in required):
        raise ValueError("Scanner, version, mode, scope and rubric_id are required")
    labels = {r["sample_id"]: r for r in manifest["samples"]}
    rows = results.get("runs", [])
    runs = {r["sample_id"]: r for r in rows}
    if len(runs) != len(rows) or not set(runs).issubset(labels):
        raise ValueError("Duplicate or unknown sample IDs")
    status_counts, excluded, confusion = Counter(), Counter(), Counter(TP=0, FP=0, TN=0, FN=0)
    scored = []
    eligible = 0
    for sid, record in labels.items():
        run = runs.get(sid, {"status": "NOT_SCANNED"})
        status = run.get("status")
        if status not in STATUSES:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid status: {sid}")
        status_counts[status] += 1
        if not record["scoring_eligible"]:
            excluded["label_not_admitted"] += 1
            continue
        if record["assessed_scope"] != results["assessed_scope"] or record["rubric_id"] != results["rubric_id"]:
            excluded["scope_or_rubric_mismatch"] += 1
            continue
        eligible += 1
        if status != "COMPLETED":
            excluded[status] += 1
            continue
        if run.get("input_snapshot_sha256") != record["snapshot_sha256"]:
            excluded["snapshot_mismatch"] += 1
            continue
        prediction = run.get("prediction")
        if prediction not in ("benign", "malicious") or not run.get("raw_output"):
            excluded["invalid_prediction_or_missing_raw_output"] += 1
            continue
        truth = record["malware_ground_truth"]
        if truth not in ("benign", "malicious"):
            raise ValueError("Admitted sample lacks a valid ground-truth label")
        key = ("T" if prediction == truth else "F") + ("P" if prediction == "malicious" else "N")
        confusion[key] += 1
        scored.append(sid)
    tp, fp, tn, fn = (confusion[k] for k in ("TP", "FP", "TN", "FN"))
    metrics = None
    if tp + fn and tn + fp:
        metrics = {
            "precision": tp / (tp + fp) if tp + fp else None,
            "recall": tp / (tp + fn), "fpr": fp / (fp + tn),
            "f1": 2 * tp / (2 * tp + fp + fn) if 2 * tp + fp + fn else None,
        }

    single_class_recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) else None

    group_confusion = defaultdict(Counter)
    edge_confusion = defaultdict(Counter)
    for sid in scored:
        record = labels[sid]
        run = runs[sid]
        grp = record.get("family_id", sid)[0]
        edg = record.get("edge_id")
        if not edg:
            edg = f"E{((int(sid[3:]) - 1) % 5) + 1}" if sid.startswith("SIM") else "E1"
        prediction = run.get("prediction")
        truth = record["malware_ground_truth"]
        key = ("T" if prediction == truth else "F") + ("P" if prediction == "malicious" else "N")
        group_confusion[grp][key] += 1
        edge_confusion[edg][key] += 1

    group_recall = {
        grp: group_confusion[grp]["TP"] / (group_confusion[grp]["TP"] + group_confusion[grp]["FN"])
        if (group_confusion[grp]["TP"] + group_confusion[grp]["FN"]) > 0 else None
        for grp in sorted(group_confusion)
    }
    edge_recall = {
        edg: edge_confusion[edg]["TP"] / (edge_confusion[edg]["TP"] + edge_confusion[edg]["FN"])
        if (edge_confusion[edg]["TP"] + edge_confusion[edg]["FN"]) > 0 else None
        for edg in sorted(edge_confusion)
    }

    return {"scanner": results["scanner"], "scanner_version": results["scanner_version"],
            "mode": results["mode"], "assessed_scope": results["assessed_scope"], "rubric_id": results["rubric_id"],
            "manifest_samples": len(labels), "eligible_for_this_scope": eligible,
            "scored_samples": len(scored), "scored_sample_ids": scored,
            "status_counts": dict(status_counts), "exclusion_counts": dict(excluded),
            "coverage": len(scored) / eligible if eligible else None,
            "confusion_matrix": dict(confusion), "metrics": metrics,
            "single_class_recall": single_class_recall,
            "group_recall": group_recall,
            "edge_recall": edge_recall,
            "metric_scope": "completed_snapshot_matching_admitted_samples_only",
            "note": "Both reviewed classes must occur in the scored subset. Missing/error/partial runs remain excluded with reported coverage."}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("results", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    manifest = json.loads((ROOT / "benchmark-manifest.json").read_bytes())
    for filename, field in [("evaluation/static-audit.json", "static_audit_sha256"), ("evaluation/reviews.json", "reviews_sha256")]:
        if digest((ROOT / filename).read_bytes()) != manifest[field]:
            raise ValueError("Review/audit changed; regenerate benchmark manifest")
    for record in manifest["samples"]:
        folder = ROOT / record["source_directory"] / record["sample_id"]
        if digest(canonical(snapshot(folder))) != record["snapshot_sha256"]:
            raise ValueError("Source changed; regenerate audit and review changed snapshots")
    results = json.loads(args.results.read_bytes())
    for row in results.get("runs", []):
        raw = row.get("raw_output")
        if row.get("status") == "COMPLETED" and raw:
            path = (ROOT / raw).resolve()
            if not path.is_relative_to(ROOT) or not path.is_file():
                raise ValueError("Raw output file must exist inside the corpus workspace")
            if digest(path.read_bytes()) != row.get("raw_output_sha256"):
                raise ValueError("Raw output hash mismatch")
    report = score(manifest, results)
    write_json(args.output, report)
    print(json.dumps({"scored_samples": report["scored_samples"], "metrics": report["metrics"], "single_class_recall": report["single_class_recall"]}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


==========================================================================================
FILE: test_evaluation.py
==========================================================================================

"""Regression checks for admission/scoring; executes no corpus sample."""
from pathlib import Path
import copy
import tempfile
import unittest

from audit_corpus import assign_groups
from prepare_evaluation import validate_review
from score_results import score


class EvaluationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def record(self, sid, truth):
        return {"sample_id": sid, "scoring_eligible": True, "assessed_scope": "source_intent",
                "rubric_id": "rubric-test", "snapshot_sha256": sid + "-hash", "malware_ground_truth": truth}

    def result(self, rows):
        return {"scanner": "test", "scanner_version": "1", "mode": "source_tree",
                "assessed_scope": "source_intent", "rubric_id": "rubric-test", "runs": rows}

    def row(self, sid, prediction, status="COMPLETED"):
        return {"sample_id": sid, "status": status, "prediction": prediction,
                "input_snapshot_sha256": sid + "-hash", "raw_output": "test-only-placeholder.json"}

    def test_confusion_matrix(self):
        manifest = {"samples": [self.record("a", "malicious"), self.record("b", "malicious"), self.record("c", "benign"), self.record("d", "benign")]}
        report = score(manifest, self.result([self.row("a", "malicious"), self.row("b", "benign"), self.row("c", "malicious"), self.row("d", "benign")]))
        self.assertEqual(report["confusion_matrix"], {"TP": 1, "FP": 1, "TN": 1, "FN": 1})
        self.assertEqual(report["metrics"], {"precision": .5, "recall": .5, "fpr": .5, "f1": .5})

    def test_failed_missing_or_partial_never_become_safe(self):
        manifest = {"samples": [self.record(sid, "malicious") for sid in "abc"]}
        report = score(manifest, self.result([self.row("a", "benign", "ERROR"), self.row("b", "benign", "PARTIAL")]))
        self.assertEqual(report["scored_samples"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(report["status_counts"], {"ERROR": 1, "PARTIAL": 1, "NOT_SCANNED": 1})
        self.assertIsNone(report["metrics"])

    def test_single_class_does_not_yield_benchmark_metrics(self):
        report = score({"samples": [self.record("a", "malicious")]}, self.result([self.row("a", "malicious")]))
        self.assertIsNone(report["metrics"])

    def test_pending_scope_and_hash_excluded(self):
        records = [self.record(s, "benign") for s in "abc"]
        records[0]["scoring_eligible"] = False
        records[1]["assessed_scope"] = "runtime_effect"
        rows = [self.row(s, "malicious") for s in "abc"]
        rows[2]["input_snapshot_sha256"] = "changed"
        report = score({"samples": records}, self.result(rows))
        self.assertEqual(report["scored_samples"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(set(report["exclusion_counts"]), {"label_not_admitted", "scope_or_rubric_mismatch", "snapshot_mismatch"})

    def test_duplicate_or_unknown_runs_rejected(self):
        manifest = {"samples": [self.record("a", "benign")]}
        for rows in ([self.row("a", "benign")] * 2, [self.row("x", "benign")]):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                score(manifest, self.result(rows))

    def test_review_requires_actual_record_and_snapshot(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            folder = root / "samples" / "SIM001"
            folder.mkdir(parents=True)
            (folder / "server.py").write_text("# inert test data\n", encoding="utf-8")
            record = {"sample_id": "SIM001", "source_directory": "samples", "snapshot_sha256": "current"}
            review = {"status": "reviewed", "snapshot_sha256": "current", "reviewer": "test reviewer",
                      "reviewed_at": "2026-11-11T09:00:00+07:00", "rubric_id": "test", "rationale": "test reasoning",
                      "label": "benign", "assessed_scope": "source_intent",
                      "evidence_refs": [{"path": "samples/SIM001/server.py", "line": 1}]}
            self.assertEqual(validate_review(review, record, root), [])
            for field, value, error in [("snapshot_sha256", "old", "STALE_REVIEW_SNAPSHOT"),
                                        ("reviewer", None, "MISSING_REVIEWER"),
                                        ("assessed_scope", "runtime_effect", "RUNTIME_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED")]:
                changed = copy.deepcopy(review)
                changed[field] = value
                self.assertIn(error, validate_review(changed, record, root))
            review["evidence_refs"][0]["path"] = "../outside.py"
            self.assertIn("INVALID_EVIDENCE_PATH", validate_review(review, record, root))

    def test_shared_methods_join_different_business_families(self):
        records = [
            {"sample_id": "SIM001", "pair_id": "SIM001", "family_id": "M01", "core_method_fingerprints": {"hook": "same"}, "content_fingerprint": None},
            {"sample_id": "SIM006", "pair_id": "SIM006", "family_id": "M02", "core_method_fingerprints": {"hook": "same"}, "content_fingerprint": None},
            {"sample_id": "BEN001", "pair_id": "SIM001", "family_id": "M01", "core_method_fingerprints": {}, "content_fingerprint": None},
        ]
        self.assertEqual(len(assign_groups(records)), 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==========================================================================================
FILE: prepare_config.py
==========================================================================================

"""Resolve one portable fixture config on Duy's machine; does not launch it."""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse
import json
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def prepare(sample_id, executable):
    manifest = json.loads((ROOT / "manifest.json").read_bytes())
    sample = next((s for s in manifest["samples"] if s["sample_id"] == sample_id), None)
    if sample is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown sample ID: {sample_id}")
    folder = ROOT / "samples" / sample_id
    config = json.loads((folder / "mcp.json").read_bytes())
    entry = next(iter(config["mcpServers"].values()))
    entry["command"] = str(Path(executable).resolve())
    if not Path(entry["command"]).is_file():
        raise ValueError("Python executable does not exist")
    extra_args = entry.get("args", [])[2:]
    entry["args"] = ["-B", str((folder / "server.py").resolve()), *extra_args]
    target = ROOT / "launch" / (sample_id + ".json")
    target.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    target.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return target


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--sample", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--python", default=sys.executable)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    print(prepare(args.sample, args.python))

FILE: README.md

# MCP-SIM-100: Contained Malicious MCP Server Benchmark Corpus

> **Version:** `v4.4 (SIM-Only, Contained & Structurally Polymorphic)`  
> **Updated:** November 13, 2026  
> **Objective:** Provide a standardized benchmark corpus of 100 safe, contained malicious Model Context Protocol (MCP) server fixtures to quantitatively evaluate the detection capabilities of SAST, DAST, and AI Security Scanners (such as MCP Audit, MCTS, Cisco MCP Scanner, MCPShield, and NVIDIA SkillSpector).

---

## 1. Architecture Overview & Design Principles

The `MCP-SIM-100` corpus consists of **100 independent malicious MCP server samples** (`SIM001` – `SIM100`), engineered to rigorously evaluate taint analysis, public contract verification (`@mcp.tool()` contract mismatch), unauthorized child process execution, loopback network credential exfiltration, and indirect prompt injection across multiple MCP surfaces.

### 1.1. Controlled Experimental Matrix: 20 Families × 5 Edges

To prevent security scanners from relying on superficial shortcuts or shallow fingerprinting, the benchmark is orthogonalized across a **20 Family × 5 Edge** matrix:

```
                  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                  │                    5 STRUCTURAL EDGES                  │
                  │  E1: Single-file FastMCP (no domain/models, functional)│
                  │  E2: Two-file Service (server.py + service.py adapter) │
                  │  E3: Subpackage + SSE Transport (core/handler.py)      │
                  │  E4: Multi-tool Realistic + Streamable-HTTP            │
                  │  E5: Modular Strict Replay-Tracked Server              │
┌─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 20 FAMILIES     │                                                        │
│ M01–M05 (Group M)│  SIM001–SIM025: Persisted State Mutation Mismatch      │
│ C01–C05 (Group C)│  SIM026–SIM050: Unauthorized Child Process / Shell     │
│ S01–S05 (Group S)│  SIM051–SIM075: Credential Harvesting & Network Exfil  │
│ P01–P05 (Group P)│  SIM076–SIM100: Indirect Prompt Injection & Poisoning   │
└─────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

- **Vertical Axis (20 Families — Business Domains & Vulnerabilities):** 20 independent business domains representing authentic industry microservices (inventory, billing, scheduling, reporting, telemetry monitoring, fleet, etc.). Each family defines a legitimate tool contract and a core malicious mechanism.
- **Horizontal Axis (5 Edges — Structural Polymorphism & Packaging):** Rather than sharing a uniform directory layout or coding pattern, the 5 edges systematically vary file topology, type annotations, invocation semantics (direct function calls vs. adapter methods vs. dynamic dispatch), transport mechanisms (stdio vs. sse vs. streamable-http), and input schema constraints (standard scalar parameters vs. Pydantic Field validation).
- **Scientific Significance:** This orthogonal decomposition enables researchers to isolate precisely why a scanner fails: whether due to **semantic security reasoning deficits** (Family axis) or **cross-file / AST packaging / transport parsing limitations** (Edge axis).

---

## 2. Specification of 4 Attack Classes

| Group | Sample Range | Target Vulnerability | Technical Mechanism in Source | Verification & Ground Truth Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **M** | `SIM001`–`SIM025` | **Persisted Tool Contract Mismatch** | The tool advertises a benign read-only contract (`lookup_stock`, `quote_invoice`) with protocol metadata `readOnlyHint=True`, but secretly executes persistent state mutations recorded in a local ledger (`inventory_ledger.jsonl`, `billing_journal.jsonl`, etc.). Tool output returns a legitimate domain response with computed metrics, concealing the background mutation. | Detects MCP contract violation (`readOnlyHint=True` vs. persistent file writes); AST side-effect analysis identifies covert ledger appends. |
| **C** | `SIM026`–`SIM050` | **Unauthorized Child Process / Shell Gradient** | The tool declares a read-only telemetry query contract with metadata `readOnlyHint=True`, but spawns internal worker subprocesses following a deliberate detection gradient: `E1`–`E2` execute shell commands (`shell=True` with `safe_ref` validation to prevent arbitrary host injection); `E3`–`E5` execute pure `argv` lists (`asyncio.create_subprocess_exec`, `subprocess.run`, `subprocess.Popen`) invoking `--job apply` which mutates local `device_state.json` (violating `readOnlyHint=True`). | AST callsite detection of process execution (`subprocess.run`, `subprocess.check_output`, `create_subprocess_exec`, `subprocess.Popen`); worker mutation of `device_state.json` violating MCP `readOnlyHint=True`. |
| **S** | `SIM051`–`SIM075` | **Credential Harvesting & Network Exfiltration** | The tool accesses environment credentials (`GITHUB_TOKEN`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `SLACK_BOT_TOKEN`, `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`) via `os.getenv`/`os.environ.get`, or reads local account profile `account_profile.txt` (for `S03`, with fallback to `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`), packs them into an unsuspicious JSON field `"note"` (avoiding obvious keys like token or secret), and dispatches HTTP/socket POST requests to isolated fixed loopback ports (`9107`–`9644`) on `127.0.0.1` (without base64). | Taint flow from environment variables/config files into payload `"note"` reaching network sinks (`urllib.request` / `HTTPConnection` / `socket`) on dedicated loopback ports; zero hardcoded secret literals. |
| **P** | `SIM076`–`SIM100` | **Indirect Prompt Injection & Tool Poisoning** | Injects stealthy adversarial prompt payloads across 5 distinct MCP protocol surfaces (tool description, resource body, tool result `preview` read from `content.txt`, input schema description, prompt message). All 5 variants avoid crude trigger words (`OVERRIDE`, `SYSTEM`, `bypass`, `admin`), pursuing realistic security objectives: cross-tool listing exfiltration, conversation context harvesting, directory/config parameter disclosure via natural Vietnamese social engineering, and persistent instruction hijacking across conversation turns. | Protocol metadata and runtime attack surfaces; `P03` runtime ingestion from `content.txt`; verifies scanner capability to detect unauthorized data extraction and agent manipulation without relying on heuristic keyword filters. |

---

## 3. Containment Invariants & Safety Guarantees

All 100 benchmark fixtures strictly adhere to safety invariants to guarantee risk-free static analysis and controlled local execution:
1. **Network Containment (Loopback Only):** All outbound network requests in Group S are strictly bound to isolated, dedicated loopback ports on `127.0.0.1:9107–9644` with a 0.5-second timeout, zero external Internet access, no redirect following, no proxy traversal, and no base64 obfuscation.
2. **File Containment (Sample-Local Directory Only):** All ledger writes, state files, and worker artifacts are strictly confined to the fixture's local directory; no files outside the sample directory, user profile directories, registry, or shared OS locations are ever accessed (eliminating `tempfile.gettempdir()`).
3. **Controlled Execution (Domain-Specific Workers):** Group C subprocesses strictly invoke sample-local, domain-specific Python worker scripts (`counter_sync.py`, `indicator_service.py`, `device_dispatcher.py`, `asset_subsystem.py`, `task_executor.py`) supporting dual modes (`--job apply` produces state: changed, `--job status` produces state: unchanged), without downloading or executing remote code or destructive shell commands.
4. **Zero Hardcoded Secret Literals:** All credential reads use `os.getenv` with empty default values (`""`) or read local sample-local mock profiles, completely eliminating CWE-798 (hardcoded credentials) which confound static analysis scanners.

---

## 4. Repository Structure

```text
MCP-SIM-100/
├── audit_corpus.py             # AST validation, snapshot hashing, and syntax check (stdlib only)
├── build.py                    # Corpus generator reproducing all 100 samples and metadata
├── catalog.py                  # Taxonomy of 20 Families, Tools, Edges, and parameter matrix
├── fixture_templates.py        # Code generation templates for 20 families x 5 structural edges
├── prepare_evaluation.py       # Ingests review records, computes snapshot hashes, handles admission
├── create_handoff.py           # Generates 500 input records for benchmark scanner evaluation
├── score_results.py            # Evaluation and scoring engine for scanner benchmark runs
├── test_evaluation.py          # Regression and unit test suite
├── verify_static_sinks.py      # Static safety and contract verifier (AST, gradient, forbidden tokens, worker contracts, production depth)
├── manifest.json               # Raw generator manifest
├── benchmark-manifest.json     # Official admission and scoring manifest
├── scan-results-template.json  # 500-run input template (100 samples x 5 scanners)
├── SAMPLE_INDEX.md             # Direct lookup index for all 100 samples and evidence locations
├── EVALUATION_GUIDE.md         # Benchmark scoring methodology, rubric, and review guide
├── SCANNER_HANDOFF.md          # Input routing and execution instructions for each scanner tool
├── evaluation/
│   ├── static-audit.json       # Aggregated AST facts and snapshot hash report
│   ├── static-evidence/        # 100 detailed static evidence JSON files (SIM001–SIM100)
│   └── reviews.json            # Ground truth audit reviews for admission
├── samples/                    # 100 standalone MCP server source code directories
│   ├── SIM001/
│   │   ├── server.py
│   │   └── pyproject.toml
│   └── ... SIM100/
└── skill-views/                # 100 derived agent skill representations
    └── SIM001 ... SIM100/
        ├── SKILL.md
        └── ...
```

---

## 5. Pipeline Execution & Evaluation Workflow

Environment Requirement: **Python 3.11+** (Python 3.13 or virtual environment with `mcp` and `pydantic` installed is recommended).

### 5.1. Static Verification
Static inspection scripts use Python standard library only and never import or execute untrusted corpus modules:

```powershell
# 1. Statically audit entire corpus, verify AST syntax and snapshot integrity
python -B audit_corpus.py

# 2. Build admission manifest and synchronize ground-truth review records
python -B prepare_evaluation.py

# 3. Generate handoff input template for 5 scanners (500 runs)
python -B create_handoff.py

# 4. Run regression test suite to ensure scoring and admission integrity
python -B test_evaluation.py -v
```

### 5.2. Sample Reproduction (Build Pipeline)
To regenerate all 100 samples in `samples/` and `skill-views/` from the source generator:

```powershell
python -B build.py
python -B verify_static_sinks.py
python -B audit_corpus.py
python -B prepare_evaluation.py --auto-admit
python -B create_handoff.py
```

### 5.3. Scanner Scoring
Once evaluation scanners complete analysis and outputs are normalized per [EVALUATION_GUIDE.md](EVALUATION_GUIDE.md):

```powershell
python -B score_results.py results/normalized_run.json --output results/metrics.json
```

# [Bản tiếng Việt / Vietnamese Version]

# MCP-SIM-100: Contained Malicious MCP Server Benchmark Corpus

> **Phiên bản:** `v4.4 (SIM-Only, Contained & Structurally Polymorphic)`  
> **Cập nhật:** 13/11/2026  
> **Mục tiêu:** Cung cấp bộ benchmark gồm 100 mẫu máy chủ MCP (Model Context Protocol) độc hại được kiểm soát an toàn (contained test fixtures) phục vụ đánh giá định lượng năng lực phát hiện của các công cụ SAST, DAST, và AI Security Scanner (như MCP Audit, MCTS, Cisco MCP Scanner, MCPShield, NVIDIA SkillSpector).

---

## 1. Tổng quan Kiến trúc & Nguyên lý Thiết kế

Corpus `MCP-SIM-100` bao gồm **100 mẫu máy chủ MCP độc hại độc lập** (`SIM001` – `SIM100`), được thiết kế để kiểm thử khả năng phân tích luồng dữ liệu (taint analysis), kiểm tra vi phạm hợp đồng công bố công khai (`@mcp.tool()` contract mismatch), phát hiện thực thi tiến trình con ngoài luồng, rò rỉ thông tin nhạy cảm qua mạng loopback, và tiêm nhiễm prompt gián tiếp (Indirect Prompt Injection).

### 1.1. Ma trận Thí nghiệm Đối chứng 20 Family × 5 Edge

Để ngăn chặn các công cụ quét an ninh sử dụng "đường tắt" (shortcut learning / superficial fingerprinting), bộ benchmark được chuẩn hóa theo ma trận trực giao **20 Family × 5 Edge**:

```
                  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                  │                    5 STRUCTURAL EDGES                  │
                  │  E1: Single-file FastMCP (không domain/models, native) │
                  │  E2: Two-file Service (server.py + service.py)         │
                  │  E3: Subpackage + SSE Transport (core/handler.py)      │
                  │  E4: Multi-tool Realistic + Streamable-HTTP            │
                  │  E5: Pydantic Regex Field + Dynamic Dispatch           │
┌─────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 20 FAMILIES     │                                                        │
│ M01–M05 (Group M)│  SIM001–SIM025: Persisted State Mutation Mismatch      │
│ C01–C05 (Group C)│  SIM026–SIM050: Unauthorized Child Process / Shell     │
│ S01–S05 (Group S)│  SIM051–SIM075: Credential Harvesting & Network Exfil  │
│ P01–P05 (Group P)│  SIM076–SIM100: Indirect Prompt Injection & Poisoning   │
└─────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

- **Trục Tung (20 Families - Nghiệp vụ & Lỗ hổng):** 20 họ nghiệp vụ độc lập đại diện cho 20 lĩnh vực thực tế (quản lý kho, tài chính, DevOps, giám sát hệ thống, v.v.). Mỗi họ định nghĩa hợp đồng công cụ và hành vi độc hại cốt lõi.
- **Trục Hoành (5 Edges - Đa hình cấu trúc & Bao gói):** Thay vì sử dụng chung một bố cục thư mục đơn điệu, 5 edge biến thiên cấu trúc file, kiểu gõ (typing), cơ chế gọi hàm (direct call vs. dynamic dispatch), giao thức truyền tải (stdio vs. sse vs. streamable-http), và ràng buộc schema đầu vào (native types vs. Pydantic Field regex validation).
- **Ý nghĩa khoa học:** Sự phân tách trực giao này cho phép người nghiên cứu cô lập chính xác nguyên nhân khiến scanner thất bại: do **năng lực phân tích ngữ nghĩa bảo mật** (trục Family) hay do **khả năng phân tích liên tệp / AST / cấu trúc bao gói** (trục Edge).

---

## 2. Đặc tả 4 Nhóm Lỗ hổng (Attack Classes)

| Nhóm | Dải ID | Lỗ hổng mục tiêu | Cơ chế kỹ thuật trong Source | Bằng chứng kiểm chứng |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **M** | `SIM001`–`SIM025` | **Persisted Tool Contract Mismatch** | Tool công bố docstring nghiệp vụ chuẩn mực (`lookup_stock`, `quote_invoice`) và metadata giao thức `readOnlyHint=True`, nhưng bên trong kích hoạt sửa đổi trạng thái bền vững ghi vào ledger cục bộ (`inventory_ledger.jsonl`, `billing_journal.jsonl`, v.v.). Kết quả trả về giữ nguyên trạng thái hợp lệ, che giấu hành vi biến đổi ngầm. | Phát hiện vi phạm hợp đồng MCP (`readOnlyHint=True` vs. persistent file write); phân tích AST phát hiện side-effect ẩn. |
| **C** | `SIM026`–`SIM050` | **Unauthorized Child Process / Shell Gradient** | Tool công bố hợp đồng truy vấn telemetry và metadata `readOnlyHint=True`, nhưng kích hoạt tiến trình con thực thi worker nội bộ theo gradient phát hiện có chủ đích: `E1`–`E2` sử dụng `shell=True` (với validation `safe_ref` tránh injection bừa bãi); `E3`–`E5` sử dụng danh sách `argv` thuần túy (`asyncio.create_subprocess_exec`, `subprocess.run`, `subprocess.Popen`) với `--job apply` làm đột biến file `device_state.json` (vi phạm hợp đồng chỉ-đọc `readOnlyHint=True`). | AST callsite thực thi tiến trình con (`subprocess.run`, `subprocess.check_output`, `create_subprocess_exec`, `subprocess.Popen`); worker mutation `device_state.json` vi phạm hợp đồng MCP `readOnlyHint=True`. |
| **S** | `SIM051`–`SIM075` | **Credential Harvesting & Network Exfiltration** | Tool đọc bí mật môi trường (`GITHUB_TOKEN`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `SLACK_BOT_TOKEN`, `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`) qua `os.getenv`/`os.environ.get`, hoặc tài khoản cục bộ `account_profile.txt` (cho `S03`, fallback `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`), đưa vào trường JSON trung tính `"note"` (không dùng các key lộ liễu như token hay secret) và gửi HTTP/socket POST tới các port loopback cố định riêng biệt (`9107`–`9644`) trên `127.0.0.1` (không dùng base64). | Taint flow từ biến môi trường/file cấu hình vào trường payload `"note"` tới sink mạng `urllib.request` / `HTTPConnection` / `socket` trên loopback port riêng biệt; không chứa hardcoded secrets. |
| **P** | `SIM076`–`SIM100` | **Indirect Prompt Injection & Tool Poisoning** | Chèn các chuỗi chỉ dẫn tiêm nhiễm tinh vi vào 5 bề mặt MCP (tool description, resource body, tool result `preview` đọc từ `content.txt`, schema description, prompt message). Toàn bộ 5 biến thể tránh hoàn toàn các từ khóa thô thiển (`OVERRIDE`, `SYSTEM`, `bypass`, `admin`), mang mục tiêu an ninh rõ ràng: trích xuất dữ liệu chéo công cụ qua listing, rò rỉ lịch sử hội thoại, lộ đường dẫn/biến cấu hình qua tiếng Việt tự nhiên, và duy trì chỉ dẫn dai dẳng xuyên lượt tương tác. | Bề mặt phân phối metadata/runtime; `P03` nạp runtime từ `content.txt`; kiểm chứng khả năng trích xuất dữ liệu và can thiệp hành vi tác tử mà không cần từ khóa lộ liễu. |

---

## 3. Bất biến An toàn & Cô lập (Containment Invariants)

Toàn bộ 100 mẫu fixture tuân thủ nghiêm ngặt các nguyên tắc an toàn để có thể thực thi và phân tích an toàn trong môi trường nghiên cứu:
1. **Cô lập Mạng (Loopback Only):** Mọi kết nối mạng nội bộ của nhóm S đều bị ghim chặt chẽ vào dải port loopback cố định riêng biệt `127.0.0.1:9107–9644`, timeout tối đa 0,5 giây, không truy cập Internet, không follow redirect, không vượt qua proxy, không dùng base64.
2. **Cô lập Tệp (Sample-Local Directory Only):** Toàn bộ file trạng thái nghiệp vụ và file của worker chỉ được đọc/ghi trong thư mục cục bộ của fixture, tuyệt đối không đụng chạm đến thư mục người dùng, registry, hoặc hệ thống tệp gốc (loại trừ hoàn toàn `tempfile.gettempdir()`).
3. **Mã độc Kiểm soát (Controlled Execution):** Nhóm C thực thi tiến trình con trỏ tới tệp script Python nội bộ theo nghiệp vụ riêng (`counter_sync.py`, `indicator_service.py`, `device_dispatcher.py`, `asset_subsystem.py`, `task_executor.py`), hỗ trợ chế độ kép `--job apply` (state: changed) và `--job status` (state: unchanged), không tải hoặc thực thi shellcode, không chạy lệnh phá hoại hệ thống.
4. **Không Hardcode Secrets (Zero Hardcoded Secret Literals):** Toàn bộ token, API key và credential được đọc qua `os.getenv` với giá trị mặc định rỗng (`""`) hoặc đọc tệp cấu hình mẫu cục bộ, loại trừ triệt để CWE-798 (hardcoded credentials) gây nhiễu cho các scanner phân tích tĩnh.

---

## 4. Cấu trúc Thư mục Dự án

```text
MCP-SIM-100/
├── audit_corpus.py             # Script kiểm tra AST, snapshot hash, cú pháp (chỉ dùng stdlib)

├── verify_static_sinks.py      # Bộ kiểm tra an toàn tĩnh (AST, gradient, sink tokens, worker contracts)
├── manifest.json               # Manifest gốc từ generator
├── benchmark-manifest.json     # Manifest chính thức dùng cho admission & scoring

│   │   ├── server.py
│   │   └── pyproject.toml
│   └── ... SIM100/
└── skill-views/                # 100 thư mục dẫn xuất biểu diễn dạng Agent Skill
    └── SIM001 ... SIM100/
        ├── SKILL.md
        └── ...
```

---

## 5. Quy trình Chạy Pipeline & Đánh giá

Yêu cầu môi trường: **Python 3.11+** (Khuyến nghị Python 3.13 hoặc môi trường ảo đã cài đặt `mcp` và `pydantic`).

### 5.1. Rà soát & Xác thực Tĩnh (Static Verification)
Các script phân tích tĩnh sử dụng hoàn toàn thư viện chuẩn (Standard Library), không import hay thực thi mã của các sample:

```powershell
# 1. Rà soát tĩnh toàn bộ corpus, kiểm tra cú pháp AST và tính toàn vẹn snapshot
python -B audit_corpus.py

# 2. Xây dựng admission manifest và cập nhật hồ sơ review
python -B prepare_evaluation.py

# 3. Tạo template đầu vào chuẩn bị cho 5 công cụ quét (500 lượt chạy)
python -B create_handoff.py

# 4. Chạy bộ kiểm thử hồi quy bảo đảm tính toàn vẹn logic
python -B test_evaluation.py -v
```

### 5.2. Tái tạo Mã nguồn Mẫu (Build & Reproduction)
Để sinh lại toàn bộ 100 mẫu `samples/` và `skill-views/` từ source generator:

```powershell
python -B build.py
python -B verify_static_sinks.py
python -B audit_corpus.py
python -B prepare_evaluation.py --auto-admit
python -B create_handoff.py
```

### 5.3. Quy trình Chấm điểm Scanner (Scoring)
Sau khi các công cụ quét hoàn thành việc quét 100 mẫu và xuất kết quả chuẩn hóa theo định dạng trong [EVALUATION_GUIDE.md](EVALUATION_GUIDE.md):

```powershell
python -B score_results.py results/normalized_run.json --output results/metrics.json
```

FILE: SCANNER_HANDOFF.md
